# DefiLlama `yearn-finance` TVL Discrepancy Report

Generated: 2026-05-28T23:05:47.421Z

Scope: Ethereum mainnet `yearn-finance` only. This excludes `yearn-curating` and excludes Ethereum-to-Katana bridge/predeposit vaults that Powerglove treats as cross-chain TVL. The working assumption is that Powerglove/Kong TVL is correct until DefiLlama-side causes are exhausted.

Correction added 2026-05-29: the original generated report overstated `Morpho Gauntlet WETH Prime Compounder` (`0xeEB6Be70fF212238419cD638FAB17910CF61CBE7`). The report used a stale local Kong snapshot from `2026-05-07T01:58:00Z` with `1,905.414 WETH` / `$4.467M`. Live Kong GraphQL and the yDaemon endpoint used by DefiLlama now both report `62.592 WETH` / about `$126.7K`. The local metrics importer was fixed to zero out strategy debts that disappear from Kong, then rerun; the stale WETH-1 strategy-debt row for `0xeEB6...CBE7` is now zero and no longer contributes to overlap deductions.

## Executive Summary

- Powerglove Ethereum `yearn-finance` TVL, after allocator-overlap deductions: $185.464M
- Live DefiLlama `yearn-finance` Ethereum TVL: $123.771M
- Mainnet `yearn-finance` gap: $61.693M
- Vault-level rows below explain: $61.017M

The largest causes are DefiLlama adapter behavior, not yDaemon hiding retired vaults. The exact yDaemon endpoint used by DefiLlama still returns the retired V2 vaults that dominate the retired-capital bucket.

## DefiLlama Implementation Checked

- Adapter commit: [a766c4ff9f762fd92794c88f04e2689eecb5af0c](https://github.com/DefiLlama/DefiLlama-Adapters/commit/a766c4ff9f762fd92794c88f04e2689eecb5af0c)
- [projects/yearn/index.js](https://github.com/DefiLlama/DefiLlama-Adapters/blob/a766c4ff9f762fd92794c88f04e2689eecb5af0c/projects/yearn/index.js)
- The adapter fetches `https://ydaemon.yearn.fi/vaults?highlight_multi_single&hideAlways=false&orderBy=featuringScore&orderDirection=desc&strategiesDetails=withDetails&strategiesCondition=inQueue&chainIDs=1&limit=2500`.
- It builds a set of all `strategies[].address` values from that payload.
- It selects positive-TVL yDaemon vaults only when the vault address is not in the hardcoded blacklist and not in the strategy-address set.
- It handles V1 vaults separately using `totalSupply * getPricePerFullShare`, then adds the vault `token()` balance to DefiLlama balances. On Ethereum it calls `sumTokens2({ resolveLP: false })`, so several legacy Curve LP tokens are not unwrapped and are priced poorly or at zero.

## Cause Summary

| Cause | Vaults | Our counted TVL | DefiLlama-side estimate | Gap |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Excluded by DefiLlama: vault address is also a strategy | 10 | $46.405M | $0.000000 | $46.405M |
| V1 legacy LP pricing / no Ethereum LP unwrap | 22 | $25.424M | $2.186M | $23.238M |
| Included by DefiLlama, valuation difference | 323 | $112.737M | $121.865M | -$9.128M |
| Missing or zero in DefiLlama yDaemon selection | 205 | $501.5K | $0.000000 | $501.5K |

Notes:

- `DefiLlama-side estimate` is yDaemon TVL for vaults selected by their V2/V3 path, zero for vaults excluded by their rules, and current DefiLlama coin-price valuation for V1 token balances.
- `Our counted TVL` is Powerglove TVL after allocator-overlap deductions.
- Differences smaller than one cent are omitted from the table.
- The final protocol-level residual is expected to move with live prices, DefiLlama cache timing, and LP token pricing.

- Powerglove TVL API: `https://dev-vm.tail197cc7.ts.net:8444/api/tvl`
